  <div class="xh-load-mark">
    <div class="xh-load-x">X</div>
    <div class="xh-load-word" id="xhLoadWord">HORIZON</div>
    <div class="xh-load-line"><span class="xh-load-fill" id="xhLoadFill"></span></div>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function(){
  "use strict";
  var loader = document.getElementById("xhLoader");
  var fill = document.getElementById("xhLoadFill");
  if (!loader || !fill) return;
  var start = Date.now();
  var duration = 1750;
  var completed = false;
  function finish(){
    if (completed) return;
    completed = true;
    fill.style.width = "100%";
    loader.className = loader.className.replace(/\bloading\b/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() + " ready";
    window.setTimeout(function(){ loader.className += " done"; }, 420);
  }
  function frame(){
    var elapsed = Date.now() - start;
    var progress = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsed / duration) * 100));
    fill.style.width = progress + "%";
    if (progress >= 100) { finish(); return; }
    window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  if (document.readyState === "complete") {
    window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  } else {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){ window.requestAnimationFrame(frame); });
  }
  window.setTimeout(finish, 3200);
})();
</script>

  <script>
  (function(){
    'use strict';


    /* Root Firebase/admin configurator bridge: no page-level hardcoded key. */
    window.XHorizonFirebaseConfigurator = window.XHorizonFirebaseConfigurator || {
      unlock:function(key, onSuccess, onError){
        var cleanKey = String(key || '').trim();
        if(!cleanKey){ return false; }
        try{
          if(window.XHorizonAdmin && typeof window.XHorizonAdmin.unlock === 'function'){
            return window.XHorizonAdmin.unlock(cleanKey, onSuccess, onError);
          }
          if(window.XHorizonMainAdmin && typeof window.XHorizonMainAdmin.validate === 'function'){
            return Promise.resolve(window.XHorizonMainAdmin.validate(cleanKey)).then(function(ok){
              if(ok && typeof onSuccess === 'function') onSuccess();
              if(!ok && typeof onError === 'function') onError(new Error('Admin key rejected'));
              return !!ok;
            });
          }
        }catch(err){
          if(typeof onError === 'function') onError(err);
          console.warn('Root Firebase admin unlock failed', err);
          return false;
        }
        console.warn('Root admin validator missing. Add XHorizonAdmin.unlock or XHorizonMainAdmin.validate in main Firebase JS.');
        if(typeof onError === 'function') onError(new Error('Root admin validator missing'));
        return false;
      },
      isConfigured:function(){
        return !!(
          (window.XHorizonAdmin && typeof window.XHorizonAdmin.unlock === 'function') ||
          (window.XHorizonMainAdmin && typeof window.XHorizonMainAdmin.validate === 'function')
        );
      }
    };

        if(document.querySelector('script[data-xhuc-root-admin="'+src+'"]')) return;
        const tag = document.createElement('script');

        tag.async = true;
        tag.defer = true;
        tag.dataset.xhucRootAdmin = src;
        tag.onerror = function(){ try{ tag.remove(); }catch(e){} };
        document.head && document.head.appendChild(tag);
      });
    }

    /* Required visual resolver: fixes startup crash that stopped default cards/admin from rendering. */
    function getCardVisual(card, variant){
      card = card || {};
      const slug = String(card.slug || slugify(card.title || '')).trim();
      const direct = String((variant === 'hero' ? (card.articleImage || card.image) : (card.image || card.articleImage)) || '').trim();
      if(direct) return direct;
      const backup = String(card.backupImage || '').trim();
      if(backup) return backup;
      if(slug && CARD_BACKUP_VISUALS && CARD_BACKUP_VISUALS[slug]) return CARD_BACKUP_VISUALS[slug];
      return NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL || HERO_NASA_VISUAL || '';
    }

    let cards = mergeDefaultCards(load(STORAGE_CARDS, defaultCards), defaultCards);
    try{ if(cards.length < defaultCards.length) cards = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultCards)); save(STORAGE_CARDS, cards); }catch(e){}
    cards = cards.map(card => Object.assign({}, card, {
      image: card.image || '',
      articleImage: card.articleImage || ''
    }));
    save(STORAGE_CARDS, cards);
    let articles = load(STORAGE_ARTICLES, createDefaultArticles(cards));
    let adminUnlocked = false;
    let articleAdminUnlocked = false;
    let currentSlug = null;
    let selectedBlockId = null;
    let draggedTool = null;
    let draggedBlockId = null;

    function load(key, fallback){
      try{ const stored = localStorage.getItem(key); return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : fallback; }
      catch(err){ console.warn('Storage load failed', err); return fallback; }
    }
    function save(key, value){
      try{ localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); } catch(err){ console.warn('Storage save failed', err); }
    }
    function mergeDefaultCards(storedCards, fallbackCards){
      const base = Array.isArray(fallbackCards) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(fallbackCards)) : [];
      const stored = Array.isArray(storedCards) ? storedCards : [];
      const byId = new Map();
      base.forEach(card => byId.set(String(card.id || card.slug), card));
      stored.forEach(card => {
        if(!card || (!card.id && !card.slug)) return;
        const key = String(card.id || card.slug);
        const original = byId.get(key);
        if(original){
          byId.set(key, Object.assign({}, original, card, { id: original.id || card.id, slug: original.slug || card.slug }));
        }else{
          byId.set(key, card);
        }
      });
      const ordered = base.map(card => byId.get(String(card.id || card.slug))).filter(Boolean);
      stored.forEach(card => {
        const key = String(card && (card.id || card.slug));
        if(key && !ordered.some(item => String(item.id || item.slug) === key)) ordered.push(card);
      });
      return ordered;
    }
    function slugify(text){return String(text||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[’']/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g,'') || 'concept'}
    function uid(prefix='b'){return prefix + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9)}
    function esc(str){return String(str ?? '').replace(/[&<>'"]/g, m => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#39;','"':'&quot;'}[m]))}
    function normalizeKey(value){return String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase()}

    /* Reference-style root Firebase/admin request. No page-level hardcoded key.
       It waits for the root admin object, then unlocks using the same validator pattern
       as the reference page. */
    function waitForRootAdmin(){
      return new Promise(resolve => {
        let tries = 0;
        const hasRoot = () => !!(
          (window.XHorizonAdmin && (typeof window.XHorizonAdmin.unlock === 'function' || typeof window.XHorizonAdmin.validate === 'function')) ||
          (window.XHorizonMainAdmin && (typeof window.XHorizonMainAdmin.unlock === 'function' || typeof window.XHorizonMainAdmin.validate === 'function')) ||
          (window.XHorizonRootAdmin && (typeof window.XHorizonRootAdmin.unlock === 'function' || typeof window.XHorizonRootAdmin.validate === 'function')) ||
          (window.XHAdmin && (typeof window.XHAdmin.unlock === 'function' || typeof window.XHAdmin.validate === 'function'))
        );
        if(hasRoot()) return resolve(true);
        const timer = setInterval(() => {
          tries += 1;
          if(hasRoot() || tries >= 34){
            clearInterval(timer);
            resolve(hasRoot());
          }
        }, 120);
      });
    }
    async function callRootAdmin(api, key, onSuccess){
      if(!api) return false;
      let unlockedByCallback = false;
      const done = () => {
        unlockedByCallback = true;
        if(typeof onSuccess === 'function') onSuccess();
      };
      try{
        if(typeof api.unlock === 'function'){
          const result = api.unlock(key, done);
          const ok = await Promise.resolve(result);
          if(ok && !unlockedByCallback) done();
          return !!ok || unlockedByCallback;
        }
        if(typeof api.validate === 'function'){
          const result = api.validate(key, done);
          const ok = await Promise.resolve(result);
          if(ok && !unlockedByCallback) done();
          return !!ok || unlockedByCallback;
        }
      }catch(err){
        console.error('X Horizon admin unlock failed:', err);
      }
      return false;
    }
    async function requestAdminUnlock(value, onSuccess, onError){
      const key = String(value || '').trim();
      if(!key) return false;
      function status(message){
        if(adminPill){
          adminPill.classList.remove('xhuc-hidden');
          adminPill.textContent = message;
        }
      }
      status('Checking admin key...');
      const ready = await waitForRootAdmin();
      if(!ready){
        const msg = 'Admin root config missing';
        status(msg);
        if(typeof onError === 'function') onError(new Error(msg));
        return false;
      }
      const roots = [window.XHorizonAdmin, window.XHorizonMainAdmin, window.XHorizonRootAdmin, window.XHAdmin].filter(Boolean);
      for(const api of roots){
        const ok = await callRootAdmin(api, key, onSuccess);
        if(ok){
          status('Admin unlocked');
          return true;
        }
      }
      const msg = 'Wrong admin key or Firebase authorization failed';
      status(msg);
      if(typeof onError === 'function') onError(new Error(msg));
      return false;
    }
    function imgError(el){
      if(!el) return;
      const slug = String(el.dataset?.xhucCardSlug || '').trim();
      const step = Number(el.dataset.xhucErrorStep || 0);
      const card = cards.find(c => c.slug === slug) || {slug, title: el.dataset?.xhucTitle || 'Human Spaceflight Concept'};
      const candidates = [
        el.dataset?.xhucBackup,
        card.backupImage,
        CARD_BACKUP_VISUALS[slug],
        card.articleImage && card.articleImage !== card.image ? card.articleImage : ''
      ].filter(Boolean).filter(src => src !== el.src);
      const src = candidates[step];
      el.dataset.xhucErrorStep = String(step + 1);
      if(src){
        el.src = src;
        el.classList.add('xhuc-fallback-img');
        return;
      }
      const emergency = buildQuantumEmergencyVisual(card, el.dataset?.xhucVariant || 'card');
      if(emergency && el.src !== emergency){
        el.onerror = null;
        el.src = emergency;
        el.classList.add('xhuc-fallback-img');
        el.style.display = 'block';
        return;
      }
      el.onerror = null;
      el.style.display = 'none';
      const parent = el.closest('.xhuc-card-media, .xhuc-hero-panel, .xhuc-block-picture');
      if(parent){ parent.style.background = '#030303'; }
    }
    window.xhucImgError = imgError;

    const xhucImageCache = new Map();
    function setImageSafe(img, src){
      if(!img) return;
      img.dataset.xhucErrorStep = '0';
      img.onerror = function(){ imgError(img); };
      img.src = src || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
    }
    function xhucHash(text){
      return String(text || '').split('').reduce((sum, ch) => ((sum * 31) + ch.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0, 7);
    }
    async function fetchNasaLibraryImage(card, variant='card'){
      const query = String(card?.imageQuery || card?.title || 'human spaceflight').trim();
      const url = `https://images-api.nasa.gov/search?media_type=image&page_size=24&q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
      try{
        const response = await fetch(url, {cache:'force-cache'});
        if(!response.ok) throw new Error('NASA image search failed');
        const data = await response.json();
        const items = (((data || {}).collection || {}).items || []);
        const candidates = items.map(item => {
          const links = Array.isArray(item.links) ? item.links : [];
          const first = links.find(l => l && l.href && /^https:\/\/images-assets\.nasa\.gov\//i.test(l.href));
          return first ? first.href : '';
        }).filter(Boolean);
        if(!candidates.length) return '';
        const offset = xhucHash(`${card?.slug || card?.title || query}-${variant}`) % candidates.length;
        return candidates[offset] || candidates[0];
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('NASA image fetch fallback used:', card?.title || query, err);
        return '';
      }
    }
    async function resolveMissionImage(card, variant='card'){
      const cacheKey = `${String(card?.slug || card?.title || 'visual').toLowerCase()}::${variant}`;
      if(xhucImageCache.has(cacheKey)) return xhucImageCache.get(cacheKey);
      const staticSrc = String(card?.image || (variant === 'hero' ? card?.articleImage : '') || '').trim();
      if(staticSrc && !staticSrc.startsWith('data:image')){
        xhucImageCache.set(cacheKey, staticSrc);
        return staticSrc;
      }
      const nasaSrc = await fetchNasaLibraryImage(card, variant);
      const finalSrc = nasaSrc || getCardVisual(card, variant) || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
      xhucImageCache.set(cacheKey, finalSrc);
      return finalSrc;
    }
    function hydrateCardImages(){
      document.querySelectorAll('#xhuc-cards img[data-xhuc-card-slug]').forEach(img => {
        const card = cards.find(c => c.slug === img.dataset.xhucCardSlug);
        if(!card) return;
        resolveMissionImage(card, 'card').then(src => setImageSafe(img, src));
      });
    }
    function hydrateHeroImage(){
      const heroImg = document.getElementById('xhuc-hero-img');
      const heroCard = {title:'Human Space Flight & Life Support', imageQuery:'NASA astronauts International Space Station spacewalk life support', backupImage:'https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/9452139338-7fb9872afd-o.jpg?w=1536', source:'NASA human spaceflight imagery'};
      resolveMissionImage(heroCard, 'hero').then(src => {
        const finalSrc = src || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
        page.style.setProperty('--xhuc-hero-img', `url('${finalSrc}')`);
        if(!heroImg) return;
        heroImg.dataset.xhucTitle = heroCard.title;
        heroImg.dataset.xhucSource = heroCard.source;
        heroImg.dataset.xhucVariant = 'hero';
        setImageSafe(heroImg, finalSrc);
      }).catch(() => {
        page.style.setProperty('--xhuc-hero-img', `url('${NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL}')`);
      });
    }

    function createDefaultArticles(cardList){
      const obj = {};
      cardList.forEach(card => {
        const articleImage = card.articleImage || card.image || getCardVisual(card, 'hero') || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
        const articleSource = card.articleSource || card.source || 'Concept visual';
        obj[card.slug] = {
          slug: card.slug,
          title: card.title,
          image: articleImage,
          source: articleSource,
          tags: card.tags,
          customImage: false,
          blocks: []
        };
      });
      return obj;
    }


    function ensureArticleMeta(card){
      if(!card) return null;
      if(!articles[card.slug]) articles[card.slug] = createDefaultArticles([card])[card.slug];
      const article = articles[card.slug];
      const cardArticleImage = card.articleImage || card.image || getCardVisual(card, 'hero') || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
      const cardArticleSource = card.articleSource || card.source || 'Concept visual';
      article.slug = article.slug || card.slug;
      article.title = article.title || card.title;
      if(!article.customImage || !article.image || article.image === FALLBACK){
        article.image = cardArticleImage;
        article.source = cardArticleSource;
      }
      article.source = article.source || cardArticleSource;
      article.tags = article.tags || card.tags || '';
      article.blocks = Array.isArray(article.blocks) ? article.blocks : [];
      return article;
    }

    function starterText(title){
      const t = String(title).toLowerCase();
      if(t.includes('core') || t.includes('fusion') || t.includes('proton') || t.includes('neutrino')) return 'This article can explain the solar interior, nuclear fusion, proton-proton reactions, neutrino production, energy transport, numerical values, and why these processes power the Sun.';
      if(t.includes('radiative') || t.includes('convective') || t.includes('photosphere') || t.includes('chromosphere') || t.includes('corona') || t.includes('atmosphere')) return 'This article can explain the Sun layer by layer, including structure, temperature, density, plasma behavior, observations, and how energy escapes into space.';
      if(t.includes('magnetic') || t.includes('sunspot') || t.includes('flare') || t.includes('ejection') || t.includes('prominence') || t.includes('filament')) return 'This article can explain magnetic field formation, active regions, plasma loops, eruptions, radiation output, space-weather effects, and major spacecraft observations.';
      if(t.includes('wind') || t.includes('heliosphere') || t.includes('heliopause') || t.includes('energetic') || t.includes('storm') || t.includes('space weather') || t.includes('aurora')) return 'This article can explain solar wind, charged particles, magnetosphere interaction, geomagnetic storms, auroras, radiation risks, and how solar activity affects Earth and spacecraft.';
      if(t.includes('parker') || t.includes('orbiter')) return 'This article can explain mission goals, instruments, orbit, major observations, solar science importance, and how the spacecraft studies the Sun close to its source.';
      if(t.includes('eclipse')) return 'This article can explain eclipse geometry, umbra and penumbra, total versus annular types, corona visibility, observation safety, and scientific importance.';
      if(t.includes('cycle') || t.includes('maximum') || t.includes('minimum')) return 'This article can explain the solar magnetic cycle, sunspot number, activity changes, solar maximum, solar minimum, and effects on space weather.';
      return 'This article can explain definition, origin, structure, physical process, observations, importance, missions, numerical facts, and summary for this solar concept.';
    }
    function unlockAdmin(){
      adminUnlocked = true;
      articleAdminUnlocked = true;
      page.classList.add('xhuc-admin-on');
      adminPill.classList.remove('xhuc-hidden'); adminPill.textContent = 'Admin unlocked';
      renderAdminSelect();
      if(currentSlug && articleMount.classList.contains('xhuc-active')) unlockArticleAdmin();
    }

    function unlockArticleAdmin(){
      articleAdminUnlocked = true;
      page.classList.add('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      articleMount.classList.add('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      const dock = document.getElementById('xhuc-editor-dock');
      if(dock) dock.classList.remove('is-collapsed');
      floatingEditor.classList.remove('xhuc-show');
      renderArticleBlocks();
    }

    function renderCards(filter=''){
      const q = normalizeKey(filter);
      const shown = cards.filter(card => !q || [card.id,card.title,card.desc,card.tags,card.source].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q));
      cardGrid.innerHTML = shown.length ? shown.map(card => `
        <article class="xhuc-card" data-slug="${esc(card.slug)}" data-card-id="${esc(card.id)}">
          <div class="xhuc-card-media">
            <span class="xhuc-card-id">${esc(card.id)}</span>
            <img src="${esc(getCardVisual(card, 'card'))}" data-xhuc-card-slug="${esc(card.slug)}" data-xhuc-title="${esc(card.title)}" data-xhuc-source="${esc(card.source || 'Concept visual')}" data-xhuc-variant="card" data-xhuc-backup="${esc(card.backupImage || '')}" alt="${esc(card.title)} visual reference" loading="lazy" onerror="xhucImgError(this)">
          </div>
          <div class="xhuc-card-body">
            <h3>${esc(card.title)}</h3>
            <p>${esc(card.desc)}</p>
            <div class="xhuc-source">${esc(card.source || 'NASA direct image visual')}</div>
            <div class="xhuc-card-actions"><button class="xhuc-btn" type="button" data-open-article="${esc(card.slug)}">Open Article</button></div>
            <div class="xhuc-card-admin-actions"><button class="xhuc-mini-btn" type="button" data-edit-card="${esc(card.id)}">Edit Card</button><button class="xhuc-mini-btn xhuc-danger" type="button" data-delete-card-inline="${esc(card.id)}">Delete</button></div>
          </div>
        </article>`).join('') : '<div class="xhuc-empty">No concept cards match this search.</div>';
      countPill.textContent = `${shown.length} / ${cards.length} Concepts`;
      renderAdminSelect();
      hydrateCardImages();
    }

    function renderAdminSelect(){
      const select = document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-select');
      if(!select) return;
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Create new card</option>' + cards.map(c => `<option value="${esc(c.id)}">${esc(c.id)} — ${esc(c.title)}</option>`).join('');
    }

    function fillCardForm(card){
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-id').value = card?.id || nextCardId();
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-title').value = card?.title || '';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-desc').value = card?.desc || '';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-img').value = card?.image || '';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-source').value = card?.source || '';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-tags').value = card?.tags || '';
    }

    function nextCardId(){
      const nums = cards.map(c => parseInt(String(c.id).replace(/\D/g,''),10)).filter(Boolean);
      const next = (Math.max(0,...nums)+1).toString().padStart(3,'0');
      return 'HSF-' + next;
    }

    function saveCardFromForm(){
      const id = document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-id').value.trim() || nextCardId();
      const title = document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-title').value.trim();
      if(!title){ alert('Add a title first.'); return; }
      const card = {
        id,
        slug: slugify(title),
        title,
        desc: document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-desc').value.trim() || 'Add a clean concept description from card admin.',
        image: document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-img').value.trim() || '',
        source: document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-source').value.trim() || 'Custom visual',
        tags: document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-tags').value.trim()
      };
      const indexById = cards.findIndex(c => c.id === id);
      const indexBySlug = cards.findIndex(c => c.slug === card.slug);
      const index = indexById >= 0 ? indexById : indexBySlug;
      if(index >= 0) cards[index] = card; else cards.push(card);
      if(!articles[card.slug]) articles[card.slug] = createDefaultArticles([card])[card.slug];
      save(STORAGE_CARDS,cards); save(STORAGE_ARTICLES,articles); renderCards(cardSearch.value); renderAdminSelect(); const sel=document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-select'); if(sel) sel.value=id;
    }

    function deleteCardById(id){
      const card = cards.find(c => c.id === id);
      if(!card) return;
      if(!confirm('Delete ' + card.title + '?')) return;
      cards = cards.filter(c => c.id !== id);
      delete articles[card.slug];
      save(STORAGE_CARDS,cards); save(STORAGE_ARTICLES,articles); fillCardForm(null); renderCards(cardSearch.value);
    }

    function deleteSelectedCard(){
      const id = document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-select').value;
      if(!id){ alert('Select a card to delete.'); return; }
      deleteCardById(id);
    }

    function openArticle(slug){
      const card = cards.find(c => c.slug === slug);
      if(!card) return;
      currentSlug = slug;
      articleAdminUnlocked = !!adminUnlocked;
      page.classList.remove('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      articleMount.classList.remove('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      editbar.classList.remove('xhuc-show'); closeInspector();
      const article = ensureArticleMeta(card);
      main.classList.add('xhuc-hidden');
      articleMount.classList.add('xhuc-active');
      articleMount.style.setProperty('--xhuc-article-img', `url('${article.image || card.image || getCardVisual(card, 'hero') || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL}')`);
      renderArticleShell(card);
      if(articleAdminUnlocked) unlockArticleAdmin();
      resolveMissionImage(card, 'hero').then(src => {
        const liveArticle = getArticle();
        if(liveArticle && !liveArticle.customImage && src && src !== FALLBACK){
          liveArticle.image = src;
          articleMount.style.setProperty('--xhuc-article-img', `url('${src}')`);
          save(STORAGE_ARTICLES, articles);
        }
      });
      window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});
    }

    function closeArticle(){
      currentSlug = null;
      articleAdminUnlocked = false;
      page.classList.remove('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      articleMount.classList.remove('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      selectedBlockId = null;
      articleMount.classList.remove('xhuc-active');
      articleMount.innerHTML = '';
      main.classList.remove('xhuc-hidden');
      editbar.classList.remove('xhuc-show');
      floatingEditor.classList.remove('xhuc-show');
      window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});
    }

    function renderArticleShell(card){
      const article = ensureArticleMeta(card);
      const articleTitle = article.title || card.title;
      const articleImage = article.image || card.image || getCardVisual(card, 'hero') || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
      const articleSource = article.source || card.source || 'NASA direct image visual';
      const articleTags = article.tags || card.tags || '';
      articleMount.style.setProperty('--xhuc-article-img', `url('${articleImage}')`);
      articleMount.innerHTML = `
        <div class="xhuc-article-hero">
          <div class="xhuc-shell xhuc-article-top">
            <button class="xhuc-back" type="button" id="xhuc-back-btn">← Back to cards</button>
            <h2 class="xhuc-article-title" id="xhuc-article-page-title">${esc(articleTitle)}</h2>
            <div class="xhuc-article-meta">
              <span class="xhuc-pill">${esc(card.id)}</span>
              <span class="xhuc-pill" id="xhuc-article-source-pill">${esc(articleSource)}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="xhuc-article-search">
              <div class="xhuc-search-wrap">
                <input id="xhuc-article-search-input" type="search" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search inside this article…">
                <button id="xhuc-clear-article-search" type="button">Search</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="xhuc-shell xhuc-article-body">
          <div class="xhuc-article-layout">
            <div id="xhuc-article-canvas" class="xhuc-article-canvas"></div>
            <aside id="xhuc-editor-dock" class="xhuc-editor-dock" aria-label="Article editor tools">
              <h3>Article Builder</h3>
              <p>Drag any content type onto the blank page. After dropping, paste URL, text, embed code, or equation code in the editor panel.</p>
              <div class="xhuc-article-save-panel" aria-label="Article save system">
                <h4>Article Save System</h4>
                <div class="xhuc-article-save-grid">
                  <label for="xhuc-article-edit-title">Article Title
                    <input id="xhuc-article-edit-title" type="text" value="${esc(articleTitle)}" placeholder="Article title">
                  </label>
                  <label for="xhuc-article-edit-image">Hero Image URL
                    <input id="xhuc-article-edit-image" type="url" value="${esc(articleImage)}" placeholder="https://...">
                  </label>
                  <label for="xhuc-article-edit-source">Visual Label
                    <input id="xhuc-article-edit-source" type="text" value="${esc(articleSource)}" placeholder="Concept visual label">
                  </label>
                  <label for="xhuc-article-edit-tags">Article Tags
                    <textarea id="xhuc-article-edit-tags" placeholder="tag one, tag two">${esc(articleTags)}</textarea>
                  </label>
                </div>
                <div class="xhuc-editor-actions">
                  <button id="xhuc-save-article-now" type="button">Save Article</button>
                  <button id="xhuc-save-article-info" type="button">Save Info</button>
                  <button id="xhuc-delete-article-content" class="xhuc-danger" type="button">Delete Article</button>
                  <button id="xhuc-reset-article-content" type="button">Reset Article</button>
                </div>
                <div id="xhuc-save-state" class="xhuc-save-state">Loaded from browser storage.</div>
              </div>
              <div class="xhuc-tool-list">
                ${toolButton('embed','Embed Code','Simulation, iframe, 3D model, calculator, or custom HTML/CSS/JS.')}
                ${toolButton('equation','Equation Embed','Paste your external equation code and preview it inside an isolated frame.')}
                ${toolButton('picture','Picture','Mission image, caption, preview placement.')}
                ${toolButton('heading','Heading','Add an editable article heading.')}
                ${toolButton('short','Short Description','Add a compact intro/summary block.')}
                ${toolButton('long','Long Context','Add a full editable explanation section.')}
                ${toolButton('tags','Tags','Add searchable article tags.')}
              </div>
              <div class="xhuc-editor-note">Use Save Article for manual saving. Drag blocks to reorder them. Every block has Edit, Copy, and Delete controls when article admin is unlocked.</div>
            </aside>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.getElementById('xhuc-back-btn').addEventListener('click', closeArticle);
      async function tryArticleSearchUnlock(){
        const input = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-search-input');
        const button = document.getElementById('xhuc-clear-article-search');
        const typed = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
        if(!typed){ articleSearch(''); return false; }
        if(button){ button.disabled = true; button.textContent = 'Checking'; }
        const ok = await requestAdminUnlock(typed, function(){
          unlockAdmin();
          unlockArticleAdmin();
        }, function(err){
          if(adminPill){ adminPill.classList.remove('xhuc-hidden'); adminPill.textContent = err && err.message ? err.message : 'Admin root config missing'; }
        });
        if(input){ input.value = ''; }
        articleSearch('');
        if(button){ button.disabled = false; button.textContent = 'Search'; }
        return !!ok;
      }
      document.getElementById('xhuc-clear-article-search').addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        tryArticleSearchUnlock();
      });
      document.getElementById('xhuc-article-search-input').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if(e.key === 'Enter'){
          e.preventDefault();
          tryArticleSearchUnlock();
        }
      });
      document.getElementById('xhuc-article-search-input').addEventListener('input', e => {
        articleSearch(e.target.value);
      });
      bindArticleManager();
      bindTools();
      renderArticleBlocks();
      resolveMissionImage(article.customImage ? {image: article.image, imageQuery: card.imageQuery || card.title, title: card.title} : card).then(src => {
        const finalSrc = src || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
        articleMount.style.setProperty('--xhuc-article-img', `url('${finalSrc}')`);
        const imgInput = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-edit-image');
        if(imgInput && !article.customImage) imgInput.value = finalSrc;
      });
    }


    function bindArticleManager(){
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('xhuc-save-article-now');
      const infoBtn = document.getElementById('xhuc-save-article-info');
      const deleteBtn = document.getElementById('xhuc-delete-article-content');
      const resetBtn = document.getElementById('xhuc-reset-article-content');
      if(saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener('click', saveArticleNow);
      if(infoBtn) infoBtn.addEventListener('click', saveArticleInfo);
      if(deleteBtn) deleteBtn.addEventListener('click', deleteCurrentArticleContent);
      if(resetBtn) resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetCurrentArticleContent);
      setSaveState('Ready');
    }

    function setSaveState(text, mode='ok'){
      const state = document.getElementById('xhuc-save-state');
      if(!state) return;
      const stamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});
      state.textContent = `${text} • ${stamp}`;
      state.classList.toggle('xhuc-ok', mode !== 'warn');
      state.classList.toggle('xhuc-warn', mode === 'warn');
    }

    function saveArticleNow(){
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!article) return;
      captureInlineEdits();
      saveArticles('Saved');
      renderArticleBlocks();
      showEditbar();
    }

    function saveArticleInfo(){
      const article = getArticle();
      const card = cards.find(c => c.slug === currentSlug);
      if(!article || !card) return;
      captureInlineEdits();
      const title = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-edit-title')?.value.trim() || card.title;
      const image = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-edit-image')?.value.trim() || card.image || getCardVisual(card, 'hero') || NASA_FALLBACK_VISUAL;
      const source = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-edit-source')?.value.trim() || card.source || 'NASA direct image visual';
      const tags = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-edit-tags')?.value.trim() || card.tags || '';
      article.title = title;
      article.image = image;
      article.source = source;
      article.tags = tags;
      article.customImage = true;
      articleMount.style.setProperty('--xhuc-article-img', `url('${image}')`);
      const titleNode = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-page-title');
      const sourceNode = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-source-pill');
      if(titleNode) titleNode.textContent = title;
      if(sourceNode) sourceNode.textContent = source;
      saveArticles('Article info saved');
    }

    function deleteCurrentArticleContent(){
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!article) return;
      if(!confirm('Delete all content blocks from this article? Card will stay safe.')) return;
      article.blocks = [];
      selectedBlockId = null;
      closeInspector();
      editbar.classList.remove('xhuc-show');
      saveArticles('Article content deleted');
      renderArticleBlocks();
    }

    function resetCurrentArticleContent(){
      const card = cards.find(c => c.slug === currentSlug);
      if(!card) return;
      if(!confirm('Reset this article info and blocks to blank default?')) return;
      articles[currentSlug] = createDefaultArticles([card])[currentSlug];
      selectedBlockId = null;
      closeInspector();
      editbar.classList.remove('xhuc-show');
      saveArticles('Article reset');
      renderArticleShell(card);
      if(articleAdminUnlocked){
        page.classList.add('xhuc-article-admin-on');
        articleMount.classList.add('xhuc-article-admin-on');
      }
    }

    function toolButton(type,title,desc){
      return `<button class="xhuc-tool" draggable="true" type="button" data-tool="${type}">${title}<small>${desc}</small></button>`;
    }

    function bindTools(){
      const dock = document.getElementById('xhuc-editor-dock');
      if(!dock) return;
      dock.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-tool').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
          draggedTool = btn.dataset.tool;
          draggedBlockId = null;
          e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', draggedTool);
          e.dataTransfer.setData('application/xhuc-tool', draggedTool);
          collapseEditorDuringDrag(true);
        });
        btn.addEventListener('dragend', () => collapseEditorDuringDrag(false));
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => addBlock(btn.dataset.tool, getArticle().blocks.length, true));
      });
    }

    function collapseEditorDuringDrag(on){
      const dock = document.getElementById('xhuc-editor-dock');
      if(!dock) return;
      if(on){ dock.classList.add('is-collapsed'); floatingEditor.classList.add('xhuc-show'); }
      else { setTimeout(() => { dock.classList.remove('is-collapsed'); floatingEditor.classList.remove('xhuc-show'); }, 140); }
    }

    function getArticle(){return articles[currentSlug];}
    function saveArticles(note='Autosaved'){
      const article = getArticle();
      if(article) article.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      save(STORAGE_ARTICLES,articles);
      setSaveState(note);
    }

    function renderArticleBlocks(){
      const canvas = document.getElementById('xhuc-article-canvas');
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!canvas || !article) return;
      let html = '';
      article.blocks.forEach((block, index) => {
        if(articleAdminUnlocked) html += dropzone(index);
        html += blockHTML(block, index);
      });
      if(articleAdminUnlocked) html += dropzone(article.blocks.length);
      canvas.innerHTML = html || '<div class="xhuc-blank-canvas" aria-label="Blank article canvas"></div>';
      bindDropzones();
      bindBlocks();
      applySelectedState();
    }

    function dropzone(index){return `<div class="xhuc-dropzone" data-drop-index="${index}">Drop content here</div>`}

    function blockHTML(block, index){
      const style = block.style || {};
      const size = parseInt(style.size || 16,10);
      const widthRaw = style.width === 'auto' ? 'auto' : parseInt(style.width || 100,10);
      const width = widthRaw === 'auto' ? 'auto' : Math.max(10, Math.min(100, widthRaw)) + '%';
      const height = Math.max(0, parseInt(style.height || 0,10));
      const align = ['left','center','right'].includes(style.align) ? style.align : 'left';
      const color = style.color || '#f4f7fb';
      const bg = style.bg || '#050505';
      const motion = style.motion && style.motion !== 'none' ? ' xhuc-motion-' + style.motion : '';
      const line = style.line ? ' xhuc-line-style' : '';
      const frameH = height ? height + 'px' : 'auto';
      const pad = Math.max(0, Math.min(90, parseInt(style.padding ?? 18,10))) + 'px';
      const radius = Math.max(0, Math.min(48, parseInt(style.radius ?? 18,10))) + 'px';
      const borderStyle = ['solid','dashed','dotted','none'].includes(style.borderStyle) ? style.borderStyle : 'solid';
      const weight = ['400','600','800','900'].includes(String(style.weight)) ? String(style.weight) : '400';
      const base = `draggable="${articleAdminUnlocked ? 'true' : 'false'}" data-block-id="${esc(block.id)}" data-block-index="${index}" data-block-type="${esc(block.type)}" data-align="${esc(align)}" style="--block-size:${size}px;--block-width:${width};--block-align:${esc(align)};--block-color:${esc(color)};--block-bg:${esc(bg)};--block-frame-height:${frameH};--block-img-height:${frameH};--block-pad:${pad};--block-radius:${radius};--block-border-style:${esc(borderStyle)};--block-weight:${weight}"`;
      const adminTools = articleAdminUnlocked ? `<span class="xhuc-block-drag-handle" title="Drag this block">Drag</span><div class="xhuc-block-actions"><button class="xhuc-block-action" type="button" data-block-edit="${esc(block.id)}">Edit</button><button class="xhuc-block-action" type="button" data-block-copy="${esc(block.id)}">Copy</button><button class="xhuc-block-action xhuc-danger" type="button" data-block-delete="${esc(block.id)}">Delete</button></div>` : '';
      const label = articleAdminUnlocked ? `<span class="xhuc-block-label">${esc(block.type)}</span>` : '';
      const emptyAdmin = msg => articleAdminUnlocked ? `<div class="xhuc-empty-block">${esc(msg)}</div>` : '';
      const content = String(block.content || '');

      if(block.type === 'heading') return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}<h2 contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${esc(content)}</h2></section>`;
      if(block.type === 'short') return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}<p contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${esc(content)}</p></section>`;
      if(block.type === 'long') return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}<div class="xhuc-long-text" contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${esc(content)}</div></section>`;
      if(block.type === 'tags'){
        const tags = content.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const tagHTML = tags.length ? tags.map(t=>`<span>${esc(t)}</span>`).join('') : emptyAdmin('Paste comma-separated tags in Edit.');
        return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}<div class="xhuc-taglist" contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${tagHTML}</div></section>`;
      }
      if(block.type === 'picture'){
        const img = content ? `<img src="${esc(content)}" data-xhuc-title="${esc(currentCard?.title || 'Article Image')}" data-xhuc-source="${esc(currentCard?.source || 'Concept visual')}" data-xhuc-variant="hero" alt="Article image" loading="lazy" onerror="xhucImgError(this)">` : emptyAdmin('Paste image URL in Edit.');
        const cap = block.caption ? `<div class="xhuc-caption" contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${esc(block.caption)}</div>` : (articleAdminUnlocked ? `<div class="xhuc-caption" contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">Add caption/source in Edit</div>` : '');
        return `<section class="xhuc-block xhuc-block-picture${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}${img}${cap}</section>`;
      }
      if(block.type === 'equation'){
        const inner = content ? `<iframe class="xhuc-frame xhuc-equation-frame" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups" srcdoc="${esc(frameDoc(content))}"></iframe>` : emptyAdmin('Paste external equation code in Edit.');
        return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}${inner}</section>`;
      }
      if(block.type === 'embed'){
        const inner = content ? `<iframe class="xhuc-frame" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups" srcdoc="${esc(frameDoc(content))}"></iframe>` : emptyAdmin('Paste simulation / iframe / embed code in Edit.');
        return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}${inner}</section>`;
      }
      return `<section class="xhuc-block${motion}${line}" ${base}>${adminTools}${label}<div contenteditable="${articleAdminUnlocked}">${esc(content)}</div></section>`;
    }

    function frameDoc(code){
      return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset='utf-8'><meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'><style>html,body{margin:0;min-height:100%;background:#05070b;color:#f8fbff;font-family:Inter,Arial,sans-serif}body{padding:18px;box-sizing:border-box}.xh-frame-note{border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);background:#090d15;border-radius:14px;padding:18px;line-height:1.5}</style></head><body>${code}</body></html>`;
    }
    function equationStarter(){return ""}
    function embedStarter(){return ""}

    function bindDropzones(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-dropzone').forEach(zone => {
        zone.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); zone.classList.add('xhuc-over'); });
        zone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => zone.classList.remove('xhuc-over'));
        zone.addEventListener('drop', e => {
          e.preventDefault(); zone.classList.remove('xhuc-over');
          const index = parseInt(zone.dataset.dropIndex,10);
          const movingBlock = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/xhuc-block') || draggedBlockId;
          const type = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/xhuc-tool') || e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain') || draggedTool;
          if(movingBlock){ moveBlockToIndex(movingBlock,index); }
          else if(type){ addBlock(type,index,true); }
          collapseEditorDuringDrag(false);
          draggedTool = null; draggedBlockId = null;
        });
      });
    }

    function bindBlocks(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-block').forEach(blockEl => {
        blockEl.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
          if(!articleAdminUnlocked) return;
          if(e.target.closest('input,textarea,select,button,[contenteditable="true"]')){ e.preventDefault(); return; }
          draggedBlockId = blockEl.dataset.blockId;
          draggedTool = null;
          e.dataTransfer.setData('application/xhuc-block', draggedBlockId);
          e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', draggedBlockId);
        });
        blockEl.addEventListener('dragend', () => { draggedBlockId = null; });
        blockEl.addEventListener('click', e => {
          if(!articleAdminUnlocked) return;
          const editBtn = e.target.closest('[data-block-edit]');
          const copyBtn = e.target.closest('[data-block-copy]');
          const delBtn = e.target.closest('[data-block-delete]');
          if(editBtn){ selectedBlockId = editBtn.dataset.blockEdit; applySelectedState(); showEditbar(); openInspector(); return; }
          if(copyBtn){ selectedBlockId = copyBtn.dataset.blockCopy; duplicateBlock(); return; }
          if(delBtn){ selectedBlockId = delBtn.dataset.blockDelete; deleteBlock(); return; }
          selectedBlockId = blockEl.dataset.blockId; showEditbar(); applySelectedState();
        });
        blockEl.addEventListener('dblclick', e => {
          if(!articleAdminUnlocked) return;
          selectedBlockId = blockEl.dataset.blockId; applySelectedState(); showEditbar(); openInspector();
        });
        blockEl.addEventListener('blur', e => {
          if(!articleAdminUnlocked) return;
          const block = getBlock(blockEl.dataset.blockId);
          if(!block) return;
          if(['heading','short','long'].includes(block.type)){
            const editable = blockEl.querySelector('[contenteditable="true"]');
            if(editable) block.content = editable.innerText.trim();
          }
          if(block.type === 'picture'){
            const cap = blockEl.querySelector('.xhuc-caption');
            if(cap) block.caption = cap.innerText.trim();
          }
          if(block.type === 'tags'){
            block.content = Array.from(blockEl.querySelectorAll('span')).map(s=>s.innerText.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
          }
          saveArticles();
        }, true);
      });
    }


    function captureInlineEdits(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-block').forEach(blockEl => {
        const block = getBlock(blockEl.dataset.blockId);
        if(!block) return;
        if(['heading','short','long'].includes(block.type)){
          const editable = blockEl.querySelector('[contenteditable="true"]');
          if(editable) block.content = editable.innerText.trim();
        }
        if(block.type === 'picture'){
          const cap = blockEl.querySelector('.xhuc-caption');
          if(cap) block.caption = cap.innerText.trim();
        }
        if(block.type === 'tags'){
          block.content = Array.from(blockEl.querySelectorAll('span')).map(s=>s.innerText.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
        }
      });
    }

    function addBlock(type, index, openEditor=false){
      if(!articleAdminUnlocked){ alert('Unlock article editor from the article search bar first.'); return; }
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!article) return;
      const block = makeBlock(type);
      article.blocks.splice(index,0,block);
      selectedBlockId = block.id;
      saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar();
      if(openEditor) setTimeout(openInspector, 30);
    }

    function makeBlock(type){
      const base = {id:uid(type), type, content:'', caption:'', style:{size:16,width:100,height:0,align:'left',color:'#f4f7fb',bg:'#050505',motion:'none',line:false,padding:18,radius:18,borderStyle:'solid',weight:'400'}};
      if(type === 'heading'){base.style.size = 40;}
      if(type === 'short'){base.style.size = 20; base.style.color = '#cbd5e1';}
      if(type === 'long'){base.style.size = 16;}
      if(type === 'picture'){base.style.bg = '#050505'; base.style.height = 0;}
      if(type === 'tags'){base.style.size = 14;}
      if(type === 'embed'){base.style.bg = '#050505'; base.style.height = 0;}
      if(type === 'equation'){base.style.bg = '#050505'; base.style.height = 0;}
      return base;
    }

    function getBlock(id){
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!article) return null;
      return article.blocks.find(b => b.id === id);
    }
    function blockIndex(id){
      const article = getArticle();
      return article ? article.blocks.findIndex(b => b.id === id) : -1;
    }
    function applySelectedState(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-block').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('xhuc-selected', el.dataset.blockId === selectedBlockId));
    }
    function showEditbar(){
      const block = getBlock(selectedBlockId);
      if(!block) return;
      editbar.classList.add('xhuc-show');
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-size').value = parseInt(block.style?.size || 16,10);
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-width').value = parseInt(block.style?.width || 100,10);
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-height').value = parseInt(block.style?.height || 0,10);
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-padding').value = parseInt(block.style?.padding ?? 18,10);
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-radius').value = parseInt(block.style?.radius ?? 18,10);
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-color').value = block.style?.color || '#f4f7fb';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-bg').value = block.style?.bg || '#050505';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-align').value = block.style?.align || 'left';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-motion').value = block.style?.motion || 'none';
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-weight').value = String(block.style?.weight || '400');
      document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-border').value = block.style?.borderStyle || 'solid';
    }
    function updateSelectedStyle(prop,value){
      const block = getBlock(selectedBlockId); if(!block) return;
      block.style = block.style || {}; block.style[prop] = value; saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar();
    }
    function moveBlock(dir){
      const article = getArticle(); const i = blockIndex(selectedBlockId); if(!article || i<0) return;
      const n = i + dir; if(n<0 || n>=article.blocks.length) return;
      const [b] = article.blocks.splice(i,1); article.blocks.splice(n,0,b); saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks();
    }
    function moveBlockToIndex(blockId,index){
      const article = getArticle();
      if(!article) return;
      const oldIndex = article.blocks.findIndex(b => b.id === blockId);
      if(oldIndex < 0) return;
      const [block] = article.blocks.splice(oldIndex,1);
      let target = Math.max(0, Math.min(article.blocks.length, index));
      if(oldIndex < index) target = Math.max(0, target - 1);
      article.blocks.splice(target,0,block);
      selectedBlockId = block.id;
      saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar();
    }
    function duplicateBlock(){
      const article = getArticle(); const i = blockIndex(selectedBlockId); if(!article || i<0) return;
      const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(article.blocks[i])); copy.id = uid(copy.type); article.blocks.splice(i+1,0,copy); selectedBlockId = copy.id; saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar();
    }
    function deleteBlock(){
      const article = getArticle(); const i = blockIndex(selectedBlockId); if(!article || i<0) return;
      if(!confirm('Delete this article block?')) return;
      article.blocks.splice(i,1); selectedBlockId = null; saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); editbar.classList.remove('xhuc-show'); closeInspector();
    }
    function editSelectedBlock(){ openInspector(); }

    function openInspector(){
      const block = getBlock(selectedBlockId); if(!block || !inspector) return;
      document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-title').textContent = 'Edit ' + block.type.toUpperCase();
      const text = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-text');
      const url = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-url');
      const caption = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-caption');
      const urlLabel = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-url-label');
      const captionLabel = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-caption-label');
      const textLabel = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-text-label');
      const w = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-width');
      const h = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-height');

      url.value = block.type === 'picture' ? (block.content || '') : '';
      caption.value = block.caption || '';
      text.value = block.type === 'picture' ? '' : (block.content || '');
      w.value = parseInt(block.style?.width || 100,10);
      h.value = parseInt(block.style?.height || 0,10);

      const isPicture = block.type === 'picture';
      const isCode = block.type === 'embed' || block.type === 'equation';
      url.style.display = isPicture ? 'block' : 'none';
      urlLabel.style.display = isPicture ? 'block' : 'none';
      caption.style.display = isPicture ? 'block' : 'none';
      captionLabel.style.display = isPicture ? 'block' : 'none';
      text.style.display = isPicture ? 'none' : 'block';
      textLabel.style.display = isPicture ? 'none' : 'block';
      if(block.type === 'heading') text.placeholder = 'Write heading text here';
      else if(block.type === 'short') text.placeholder = 'Write short description here';
      else if(block.type === 'long') text.placeholder = 'Write long article context here';
      else if(block.type === 'tags') text.placeholder = 'Paste tags separated by commas';
      else if(isCode) text.placeholder = 'Paste external HTML / CSS / JS / iframe / equation code here';
      else text.placeholder = 'Write content here';
      inspector.classList.add('xhuc-show');
      setTimeout(() => (isPicture ? url : text).focus(), 40);
    }
    function closeInspector(){ if(inspector) inspector.classList.remove('xhuc-show'); }
    function applyInspector(){
      const block = getBlock(selectedBlockId); if(!block) return;
      const text = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-text');
      const url = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-url');
      const caption = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-caption');
      const w = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-width');
      const h = document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-height');
      if(block.type === 'picture'){
        block.content = url.value.trim();
        block.caption = caption.value.trim();
      } else {
        block.content = text.value;
      }
      block.style = block.style || {};
      block.style.width = Math.max(10, Math.min(100, parseInt(w.value || block.style.width || 100,10)));
      block.style.height = Math.max(0, Math.min(1600, parseInt(h.value || 0,10)));
      saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar(); closeInspector();
    }

    function articleSearch(value){
      const q = normalizeKey(value);
      document.querySelectorAll('.xhuc-block').forEach(el => {
        const text = el.innerText.toLowerCase();
        const match = !q || text.includes(q) || (el.dataset.blockType || '').includes(q);
        el.style.display = match ? '' : 'none';
        el.classList.toggle('xhuc-search-hit', !!q && match);
      });
    }

    floatingEditor.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const dock = document.getElementById('xhuc-editor-dock');
      if(dock){ dock.classList.remove('is-collapsed'); floatingEditor.classList.remove('xhuc-show'); }
    });

    async function tryCardSearchUnlock(){
      const typed = cardSearch.value.trim();
      const button = document.getElementById('xhuc-clear-search');
      if(!typed){ renderCards(''); return false; }
      if(button){ button.disabled = true; button.textContent = 'Checking'; }
      const ok = await requestAdminUnlock(typed, function(){
        unlockAdmin();
      }, function(err){
        if(adminPill){ adminPill.classList.remove('xhuc-hidden'); adminPill.textContent = err && err.message ? err.message : 'Admin root config missing'; }
      });
      cardSearch.value = '';
      renderCards('');
      if(button){ button.disabled = false; button.textContent = 'Search'; }
      return !!ok;
    }
    cardSearch.addEventListener('input', e => { renderCards(e.target.value); });
    cardSearch.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if(e.key === 'Enter'){
        e.preventDefault();
        tryCardSearchUnlock();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('xhuc-clear-search').addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.preventDefault();
      tryCardSearchUnlock();
    });
    cardGrid.addEventListener('click', e => {
      const editBtn = e.target.closest('[data-edit-card]');
      if(editBtn){
        const card = cards.find(c => c.id === editBtn.dataset.editCard);
        if(card){
          const sel=document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-select');
          if(sel) sel.value=card.id;
          fillCardForm(card);
          document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-card-panel').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'});
        }
        return;
      }
      const delBtn = e.target.closest('[data-delete-card-inline]');
      if(delBtn){ deleteCardById(delBtn.dataset.deleteCardInline); return; }
      const btn = e.target.closest('[data-open-article]');
      if(btn) openArticle(btn.dataset.openArticle);
    });

    document.getElementById('xhuc-admin-select').addEventListener('change', e => {
      const card = cards.find(c => c.id === e.target.value);
      fillCardForm(card || null);
    });
    document.getElementById('xhuc-new-card').addEventListener('click', () => fillCardForm(null));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-save-card').addEventListener('click', saveCardFromForm);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-delete-card').addEventListener('click', deleteSelectedCard);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-reset-cards').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if(confirm('Reset all cards and default articles to the corrected Human Space Flight & Life Support list?')){
        cards = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultCards));
        articles = createDefaultArticles(cards);
        save(STORAGE_CARDS,cards); save(STORAGE_ARTICLES,articles); renderCards(''); fillCardForm(null);
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-size').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('size', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-width').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('width', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-height').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('height', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-padding').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('padding', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-radius').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('radius', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-color').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('color', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-bg').addEventListener('input', e => updateSelectedStyle('bg', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-align').addEventListener('change', e => updateSelectedStyle('align', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-motion').addEventListener('change', e => updateSelectedStyle('motion', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-weight').addEventListener('change', e => updateSelectedStyle('weight', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-border').addEventListener('change', e => updateSelectedStyle('borderStyle', e.target.value));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-line').addEventListener('click', () => { const b=getBlock(selectedBlockId); if(!b)return; b.style.line = !b.style.line; saveArticles(); renderArticleBlocks(); showEditbar(); });
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-up').addEventListener('click', () => moveBlock(-1));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-down').addEventListener('click', () => moveBlock(1));
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-edit').addEventListener('click', editSelectedBlock);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-copy').addEventListener('click', duplicateBlock);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-edit-delete').addEventListener('click', deleteBlock);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-save').addEventListener('click', applyInspector);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeInspector);
    document.getElementById('xhuc-inspector-close').addEventListener('click', closeInspector);

    const xhucHeroImgNode = document.getElementById('xhuc-hero-img');
    if(xhucHeroImgNode){ xhucHeroImgNode.addEventListener('error', e => imgError(e.target)); }

    function forceCardsVisibleAtStart(){
      if(cardSearch) cardSearch.value = '';
      if(cardGrid){
        cardGrid.style.display = 'grid';
        cardGrid.style.visibility = 'visible';
        cardGrid.style.opacity = '1';
      }
      renderCards('');
      if(cardGrid && !cardGrid.querySelector('.xhuc-card')){
        cards = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultCards));
        articles = createDefaultArticles(cards);
        save(STORAGE_CARDS, cards);
        save(STORAGE_ARTICLES, articles);
        renderCards('');
      }
    }
    forceCardsVisibleAtStart();
    requestAnimationFrame(forceCardsVisibleAtStart);
    window.addEventListener('load', forceCardsVisibleAtStart, {once:true});
    hydrateHeroImage();
    fillCardForm(null);
  })();
  </script>
